TL;DR: 本文提出XDomainBench基准，用于诊断LLM在跨学科科学推理中的组合泛化能力。通过8,598个交互式会话、20个领域和4类任务，系统评估发现LLM存在推理崩溃现象，根源在于领域组合带来的直接难度增加和交互放大的间接错误累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注单轮受限场景，无法捕捉真实交互式科学工作流中LLM的能力边界，因此需要构建一个能系统测试从单学科到跨学科组合泛化能力的诊断基准。

Method: 形式化定义了组合顺序和混合结构，构建了包含8,598个交互会话、覆盖20个领域和4类任务类型的XDomainBench基准，设计了8种轨迹模式来模拟真实AI4S场景。

Result: 大规模评估显示LLM存在系统性推理崩溃：随着组合顺序增加，性能持续下降。根源包括领域组合导致的直接难度增加，以及轨迹模式触发的错误累积、推理中断和领域混淆等交互放大失败。

Conclusion: LLM在跨学科交互式科学推理中存在组合泛化能力不足的问题，表现为推理崩溃，其成因包括直接难度和间接交互放大两方面因素。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for knowledge synthesis, yet their capacity for compositional generalization in scientific knowledge remains under-characterized. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on single-turn restricted scenarios, failing to capture the capability boundaries exposed by real-world interactive scientific workflows. To address this, we introduce XDomainBench, a diagnostic benchmark for interactive interdisciplinary scientific reasoning. We formalize the composition order and mixture structure to enable systematic stress-testing from single-discipline to inter-disciplinary, comprising 8,598 interactive sessions across 20 domains and 4 task categories, with 8 realistic trajectory patterns covering difficulty and domain-mixture dynamics, simulating real AI4S scenarios. Large-scale evaluation of LLMs reveals a systematic reasoning collapse as composition order increases, stemming from two root causes: (i) direct difficulty increases induced by domain composition, and (ii) indirect interaction-amplified failures where trajectory patterns trigger error accumulation, reasoning breaks, and domain confusion, ultimately leading to session collapse.

</details>


### [110] [Probabilistic Verification of Recurrent Neural Networks for Single and Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14758)
*Luca Marzari,Enrico Marchesini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RNN-ProVe，一种用于RNN策略的轻量级概率验证框架，通过策略驱动采样估计隐藏状态可行集，并给出带统计误差边界的违规行为概率保证，在部分可观察单智能体和多智能体任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RNN验证工具要么依赖限制性假设，要么对隐藏状态空间进行粗糙过近似，导致结果过于保守或无法下结论，缺乏对RNN策略中不良行为发生可能性的定量概率估计。

Method: 采用策略驱动采样来近似训练策略下的可行隐藏状态集，并结合统计误差界，产生有界误差、高置信度的行为违规概率估计。

Result: 在部分可观察单智能体和协作多智能体任务中，RNN-ProVe比现有工具提供更定量、更可行感知的概率保证，并能扩展到循环和多智能体场景。

Conclusion: RNN-ProVe为RNN策略验证提供了一种可行的概率性替代方案，在保持可扩展性的同时克服了传统过度保守近似的问题。

Abstract: History-dependent policies induced by recurrent neural networks (RNNs) rely on latent hidden state dynamics, making verification in partially observable reinforcement learning (RL) challenging. Existing RNN verification tools typically rely on restrictive modeling assumptions or coarse over-approximations of the hidden state space, which can lead to overly conservative or inconclusive results. We propose $\textbf{RNN}$ $\textbf{Pro}$babilistic $\textbf{Ve}$rification ($\texttt{RNN-ProVe}$), a probabilistic framework that $\textit{estimates the likelihood}$ of undesired behaviors in RNN-based policies. $\texttt{RNN-ProVe}$ uses policy-driven sampling to approximate the set of hidden states that are feasible under a trained policy, and derives statistical error bounds to produce bounded-error, high-confidence estimates of behavioral violations. Experiments on partially observable single-agent and cooperative multi-agent tasks show that $\texttt{RNN-ProVe}$ yields more quantitative, feasibility-aware probabilistic guarantees than existing tools, while scaling to recurrent and multi-agent settings.

</details>


### [111] [AI Outperforms Humans in Personalized Image Aesthetics Assessment via LLM-Based Interviews and Semantic Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14761)
*Yoshia Abe,Tatsuya Daikoku,Yasuo Kuniyoshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个集成深度学习与大语言模型的系统，通过半结构化访谈主动收集个人审美偏好，结合图像的低层和高层特征进行审美评估预测。实验表明该系统在所有对比中表现最优，预测误差甚至小于个体自身的时间变异性，引发AI是否比人类更能解读人类审美感知的思考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型仅能提取图像的客观低层特征，但审美偏好具有主观性和个体依赖性，准确预测需要提取高层次语义特征并主动收集目标个体的偏好信息。

Method: 开发了一个集成深度学习与大语言模型的系统：利用基于LLM的半结构化访谈主动收集个体审美偏好，结合低层视觉特征和高层语义特征进行审美评估预测。

Result: 所提系统优于传统系统、人类预测者以及目标个体在时间间隔后的自我重评；在高评分图像上表现尤为突出；预测误差小于个体自身的时间变异性。

Conclusion: AI可能比他人甚至个体未来的自我更能捕捉特定时间点的个人审美偏好，引发了AI能否比人类自身更深入解读人类审美感知的新问题。

Abstract: Accurately predicting individual aesthetic evaluation for images is a fundamental challenge for AI. Various deep learning (DL)-based models have been proposed for this task, training on image evaluation data to extract objective low-level features. However, aesthetic preferences are inherently subjective and individual-dependent. Accurate prediction thus requires the extraction of high-level semantic features of images and the active collection of preference information from the target individual. To address this issue, we focus on the utility of Large Language Models (LLMs) pretrained on vast amounts of textual data, and develop an integrated DL-LLM system. The system actively elicits aesthetic preferences through LLM-based semi-structured interviews and predicts aesthetic evaluation by leveraging both low-level and high-level features. In our experiments, we compare the proposed system against conventional systems, human predictors, and the target individual's own re-evaluations after a certain time interval. Our results show that the proposed system outperforms all of them, with particularly strong performance on highly-rated images. Moreover, the prediction error of the proposed system is smaller than within-person variability, while human predictors show the largest error, likely due to the influence of their own aesthetic values. These results suggest that AI may be better positioned than others or one's future self to capture individual aesthetic preferences at a given point. This opens a new question of whether AI could serve as a deeper interpreter of human aesthetic sensibility than humans themselves.

</details>


### [112] [MediaClaw: Multimodal Intelligent-Agent Platform Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14771)
*Shaoan Zhao,Huanlin Gao,Qiang Hui,Ting Lu,Xueqiang Guo,Yantao Li,Xinpei Su,Fuyuan Shi,Chao Tan,Fang Zhao,Kai Wang,Shiguo Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MediaClaw是一个基于OpenClaw生态构建的多模态Agent平台，采用统一抽象、插件化扩展和工作流编排三层架构，旨在解决AIGC落地中的能力碎片化、接口异构、流程割裂和高品质工作流复用难等工程痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决AIGC在实际部署中面临的能力碎片化、异构接口、生产流程割裂以及高质量工作流难以复用等工程痛点。

Method: 采用三层架构设计：统一抽象层（将全品类AIGC能力抽象为统一调用模型）、插件化扩展层（支持热插拔能力扩展）、工作流编排层（通过面向任务的Skill将复杂生产流程转化为可复用的工作流资产）。

Result: 构建了MediaClaw多模态Agent平台，实现了AIGC能力的统一抽象、热插拔扩展和可复用工作流编排。

Conclusion: 为构建多模态能力平台提供了可复用的工程实践参考，重点阐述了架构设计理念、核心能力模型设计逻辑以及关键工程权衡。

Abstract: MediaClaw is a multimodal agent platform built on the OpenClaw ecosystem. Its core design follows a three-layer architecture of unified abstraction, pluginized extension, and workflow orchestration. The system is intended to address practical deployment pain points in AIGC adoption, including fragmented capabilities, heterogeneous interfaces, disconnected production processes, and limited reuse of high-quality production workflows. \system{} abstracts full-category AIGC capabilities into a unified invocation model, uses plugins to support hot-pluggable capability expansion, and uses task-oriented Skills to turn complex production processes into reusable workflow assets. This report focuses on the architectural design philosophy of MediaClaw, the design logic of its core capability model, and the key engineering trade-offs in implementation. It aims to provide reusable practical reference for building multimodal capability platforms.

</details>


### [113] [Identifying Culprits Through Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient Deep Learning Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14774)
*Lata B T,Savitha N J*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于深度强化学习DDPG算法的犯罪者识别方法，在犯罪现场材料、证人陈述和嫌疑人资料数据集上训练，实现了95%的识别准确率，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刑事调查依赖有限的数据分析，难以从复杂数据集中有效识别犯罪者，且存在较高的假阳性和假阴性问题，亟需一种更优的方法。

Method: 采用Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG)深度强化学习算法，使用犯罪现场材料、证人陈述和嫌疑人资料的数据集进行训练，通过特征提取最大化识别犯罪者的可能性，同时降低噪声和无关数据的影响。

Result: DDPG方法实现了95%的犯罪者识别准确率，显著优于其他几种现有方法。

Conclusion: 基于DDPG的深度强化学习方法在犯罪识别任务上展现出优异性能，能有效降低误报和漏报，为刑事调查提供了更高效的技术手段。

Abstract: In the world of AI and advanced technologies investigation aspects identification of a crime or criminal plays a major problem. In this research we focus on a Conventional ways of implicating criminal investigations usually rely on limited data analysis. Finding an optimal and efficient method that will effectively identify criminals from complex datasets and minimise false positives and false negatives is the considered as a challenge. The main novelty approach of this work is based on the deep learning algorithm Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG) is presented in this paper. We train the DDPG model with a dataset of crime scene material, witness statements and suspect profiles. The algorithm uses features to maximise the likelihood of identifying the offender while minimising the noise impact and irrelevant data. We show the efficacy of the proposed method, where DDPG identified criminals with an amazing accuracy of 95% than other several existing methods.

</details>


### [114] [A Heterogeneous Temporal Memory Governance Framework for Long-Term LLM Persona Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14802)
*Zhao Yang,Wang Huan,Li Yingshuo,Tu Haomiao,Lin Hujite*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARPM是一个外部时序记忆治理框架，通过分离静态知识记忆与动态对话经验记忆，结合向量检索、BM25、RRF融合、双时序重排序、时序证据阅读和受控分析协议，解决大模型在长程交互中的事实丢失、时间线混乱、人格漂移和稳定性下降问题。实验表明该方法在50轮问答、高噪声基底和跨模型切换等场景下显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在长程对话中面临事实丢失、时间线混乱、人格漂移和稳定性下降等问题，尤其在噪声知识库、上下文清除和跨模型迁移场景下更为严重。现有方法要么将人格一致性编码到模型权重中，要么仅依赖长上下文，缺乏可追溯、可审计、可转移的治理方案。

Method: ARPM提出外部时序记忆治理框架，核心包括：(1) 分离静态知识记忆与动态对话经验记忆；(2) 结合向量检索、BM25、RRF融合、双时序重排序、时序证据阅读进行证据检索；(3) 采用受控分析协议进行证据验证与答案绑定。该方法将对话连续性视为可追溯、可审计、可转移的治理问题。

Result: 实验一（50轮QA）：信噪比1:5时CSV召回准确率54.0%，人工审核提升至100.0%；1:200+时CSV召回率44.0%，人工审核80.0%。实验二（消融）：禁用对话历史检索导致严格准确率从100%降至66.7%，禁用BM25降至80.0%。实验三（510万字噪声基底+上下文清除+多模型切换）：ARPM保持语义连续性、边界连续性和人格一致性，但暴露协议合规性弱的限制。

Conclusion: 长程人格一致性可以分解为可治理的组件，并以白盒方式评估。自动规则在支持证据进入提示后会低估召回率，纯语义检索不足以支撑修正与追溯，外部记忆治理框架是解决长程对话连续性问题的有效路径。

Abstract: Large language models often suffer from fact loss, timeline confusion, persona drift, and reduced stability during long-range interaction, especially under high-noise knowledge bases, context clearing, and cross-model transfer. To address these issues, we introduce ARPM, an external temporal memory governance framework for long-term dialogue. ARPM separates static knowledge memory from dynamic dialogue experience memory and combines vector retrieval, BM25, RRF fusion, dual-temporal reranking, chronological evidence reading, and a controlled analysis protocol for evidence verification and answer binding. Unlike approaches that encode persona consistency into model weights or rely only on long context, ARPM treats continuity as a traceable, auditable, and transferable governance problem. Using engineering logs, we conduct three experiments. First, in a 50-round question-answering setting, we compare signal-to-noise ratios of 1:5 and 1:200+, and distinguish CSV auto-judgment from manual review. Under 1:5, CSV recall accuracy is 54.0%, while manual review raises it to 100.0%. Under 1:200+, the values are 44.0% and 80.0%. These results show that automatic rules can underestimate recall after supporting evidence enters the prompt. Second, ablation results show that dialogue history retrieval is necessary for recent continuity: disabling it reduces strict accuracy from 100% to 66.7%, and disabling BM25 reduces it to 80.0%, indicating that pure semantic retrieval is insufficient for correction and tracing. Third, under a 5.1-million-character noise substrate, periodic context clearing, and multi-model handoff, ARPM maintains semantic continuity, boundary continuity, and persona consistency, while exposing limits caused by weak protocol compliance. These findings show that long-term persona consistency can be decomposed into governable components and evaluated in a white-box manner.

</details>


### [115] [Interestingness as an Inductive Heuristic for Future Compression Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14831)
*Vincent Herrmann,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了"趣味性"概念——即前瞻性识别哪些任务或数据具有未来进步潜力的能力，将其定义为未来压缩进步的归纳启发式。通过Kolmogorov复杂性和算法统计工具，在长度、算法和速度先验下分析复杂性-运行时剖面，证明趣味性的归纳性质在理论上可行且经验上成立。研究发现预期未来进步与最近突破的新近度呈指数依赖，算法先验比长度先验更乐观，预期发现呈二次增长。实验结果在三种通用计算范式上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进系统面临的一个瓶颈是"趣味性"挑战——即前瞻性识别哪些任务或数据具有未来进步潜力的能力，这是实现系统持续自我改进的关键。

Method: 形式化趣味性为未来压缩进步的归纳启发式，利用Kolmogorov复杂性和算法统计理论，在长度先验、算法先验和速度先验下分析复杂性-运行时剖面，并证明相关性质。

Result: 预期未来进步与最近突破的新近度呈指数依赖关系；算法先验比长度先验更乐观，预期发现量呈二次增长；实验结果在三种通用计算范式上得到验证。

Conclusion: 趣味性的归纳性质（过去进步能预示未来发现的能力）在理论上可行且经验上成立，为递归自我改进系统的实现提供了理论基础。

Abstract: One of the bottlenecks on the way towards recursively self-improving systems is the challenge of interestingness: the ability to prospectively identify which tasks or data hold the potential for future progress. We formalize interestingness as an inductive heuristic for future compression progress and investigate its predictability using tools from Kolmogorov Complexity and Algorithmic Statistics. By analyzing complexity-runtime profiles under Length, Algorithmic, and Speed priors, we demonstrate that the inductive property of interestingness -- the capacity for past progress to signal future discovery -- is theoretically viable and empirically supported. We prove that expected future progress depends exponentially on the recency of the last observed breakthrough. Furthermore, we show that the Algorithmic Prior is significantly more optimistic than the Length Prior, yielding a quadratic increase in expected discovery for the same observed profile. These findings are experimentally confirmed across three diverse universal computational paradigms.

</details>


### [116] [Holistic Evaluation and Failure Diagnosis of AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14865)
*Netta Madvil,Gilad Dym,Alon Mecilati,Edo Dekel,Jonatan Liberman,Rotem Brazilay,Liron Schliesser,Max Svidlo,Shai Nir,Orel Shalom,Yaron Friedman,David Connack,Amos Rimon,Philip Tannor,Shir Chorev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个结合自顶向下智能体级诊断与自底向上跨度级评估的智能体评估框架，通过将分析分解为独立的逐跨度评估，实现对任意长度轨迹的扩展，并在TRAIL基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估存在不足：结果性指标只能报告成功或失败，无法解释原因；过程级方法难以将错误类型与其在长结构化轨迹中的精确位置关联起来。

Method: 提出一个双层次评估框架：自上而下进行智能体级诊断，自底向上进行跨度级评估，将分析分解为独立的逐跨度评估，每个跨度生成独立的判据和推理，可扩展至任意长度轨迹。

Result: 在TRAIL基准上，GAIA和SWE-Bench的所有指标均达到SOTA；类别F1相对提升最高达38%，定位精度最高提升3.5倍，联合定位-分类精度最高提升12.5倍；在错误类别覆盖上领先其他评估器；相同的前沿模型在该框架内的定位精度比作为整体评判器高出数倍。

Conclusion: 评估方法学而非模型能力是当前智能体评估的瓶颈所在，该框架通过结构化分解有效提升了评估的精度和可解释性。

Abstract: AI agents execute complex multi-step processes, but current evaluation falls short: outcome metrics report success or failure without explaining why, and process-level approaches struggle to connect failure types to their precise locations within long, structured traces. We present a holistic agent evaluation framework that pairs top-down agent-level diagnosis with bottom-up span-level evaluation, decomposing analysis into independent per-span assessments. This decomposition scales to traces of arbitrary length and produces span-level rationales for each verdict. On the TRAIL benchmark, our framework achieves state-of-the-art results across all metrics on both GAIA and SWE-Bench, with relative gains over the strongest prior baselines of up to 38% on category F1, up to 3.5x on localization accuracy, and up to 12.5x on joint localization-categorization accuracy. Per-category analysis shows our framework leading in more error categories than any other evaluator. Notably, the same frontier model achieves several times higher localization accuracy when used inside our framework than as a monolithic judge over the full trace, showing that evaluation methodology, not model capability, is the bottleneck.

</details>


### [117] [Emotion-Attended Stateful Memory (EASM):The Architecture for Hyper-Personalization at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14833)
*Vineet Kotecha,Vansh Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种情感关注的状态记忆架构，通过长期历史、情感信号和推断意图动态构建用户特定对话上下文。在30次跨6类情感场景的非脚本对话A/B测试中，该架构在记忆扎根（提升95%）、计划清晰度（57%）和情感验证（34%）上显著优于无状态基线，即使在悲伤、痛苦等情感对抗对话中表现依然稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型系统在会话间保持无状态，无法实现跨会话的个性化交互。虽然RAG和微调改进了知识访问和领域能力，但它们未能建立对单个用户的持久理解。

Method: 提出一种情感关注的状态记忆架构，在推理时利用长期对话历史、情感信号和推断意图动态构建用户特定的对话上下文。采用同一底层语言模型进行受控A/B测试，在30次涵盖6类情感类别的非脚本对话中进行评估。

Result: 记忆增强条件在所有评估场景中一致优于无状态基线。最大改进体现在记忆扎根（95%）、计划清晰度（57%）和情感验证（34%），且在涉及悲伤、痛苦和不确定性的情感对抗对话中结果保持稳定。

Conclusion: 有状态情感记忆可能构成超个性化AI系统的基础基础设施层，但需要在更大规模和更多样化的评估中进一步验证。

Abstract: Current language model systems remain fundamentally stateless across sessions, limiting their ability to personalize interactions over time. While retrieval-augmented generation and fine-tuning improve knowledge access and domain capability, they do not enable persistent understanding of individual users. We propose an emotion-attended stateful memory architecture that dynamically constructs user-specific conversational context using long-term history, emotional signals, and inferred intent at inference time. To evaluate its impact, we conducted a controlled A/B study across thirty non-scripted conversations spanning six emotionally distinct categories using the same underlying language model in both conditions. The memory-enriched condition consistently outperformed the stateless baseline across all evaluated scenarios. The largest gains were observed in memory grounding (95% improvement), plan clarity (57%), and emotional validation (34%). Results remained consistent even in emotionally adversarial conversations involving grief, distress, and uncertainty. These findings suggest that stateful emotional memory may represent a foundational infrastructure layer for hyper-personalized AI systems, though broader validation across larger and more diverse evaluations remains necessary

</details>


### [118] [Explainable Detection of Depression Status Shifts from User Digital Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14995)
*Loris Belcastro,Francesco Gervino,Fabrizio Marozzo,Domenico Talia,Paolo Trunfio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个可解释框架，通过结合多种BERT模型和LLM，从用户数字轨迹中检测和分析抑郁相关的状态变化，生成可读性报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户每天产生的带时间戳的数字痕迹（如社交媒体帖子、聊天记录）反映了其心理状态的变化，现有方法缺乏对心理信号随时间演变的可解释分析。

Method: 组合多个BERT模型提取情感、情绪和抑郁严重程度等多维信号，按时间聚合构建用户级轨迹，识别有意义的变化点，并集成LLM生成简洁的人类可读报告。

Result: 在两个社交媒体数据集上，该方法比直接使用LLM报告产生更连贯和信息量更大的摘要，在用户历史覆盖率、时间连贯性和变化点敏感性方面表现更优。消融实验验证了各组件（尤其是时间建模和分段）的贡献。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了心理信号随时间演变的可解释视角，支持研究和决策，但不旨在进行临床诊断。

Abstract: Every day, users generate digital traces (e.g., social media posts, chats, and online interactions) that are inherently timestamped and may reflect aspects of their mental state. These traces can be organized into temporal trajectories that capture how a user's mental health signals evolve, including phases of improvement, deterioration, or stability. In this work, we propose an explainable framework for detecting and analyzing depression-related status shifts in user digital traces. The approach combines multiple BERT-based models to extract complementary signals across different dimensions (e.g., sentiment, emotion, and depression severity). Such signals are then aggregated over time to construct user-level trajectories that are analyzed to identify meaningful change points. To enhance interpretability, the framework integrates a large language model to generate concise and human-readable reports that describe the evolution of mental-health signals and highlight key transitions. We evaluate the framework on two social media datasets. Results show that the approach produces more coherent and informative summaries than direct LLM-based reporting, achieving higher coverage of user history, stronger temporal coherence, and improved sensitivity to change points. An ablation study confirms the contribution of each component, particularly temporal modeling and segmentation. Overall, the method provides an interpretable view of mental health signals over time, supporting research and decision making without aiming at clinical diagnosis.

</details>


### [119] [A Deterministic Agentic Workflow for HS Tariff Classification: Multi-Dimensional Rule Reasoning with Interpretable Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14857)
*Yu Zhang,Dongjiang Zhuang,Qu Zhou,Zheng Huang,Junhe Wu,Jing Cao,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性智能体工作流用于HS海关编码分类，采用固定控制流替代自主规划，将大模型调用限制在狭窄阶段，在六位码级别达到64.2% top-1准确率。通过两阶段人工审核发现HSCodeComp基准中部分真实标签可能偏离HS通用规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HS编码分类是高风险的专家级任务，需要满足材料、形态、功能、本质特征、部分与整体边界、具体列名与残留标题等多维优先级规则。端到端大模型提示会先解决某一维度而忽视其他维度的优先级约束，导致分类错误。

Method: 采用确定性智能体工作流：控制流固定，大模型调用仅用于狭窄阶段，内部保留反思和验证作为局部机制。架构结合离线知识工程（中文HS关税）与在线六阶段流水线，每个决策分解为阶段式结构化输出并逐字引用相关章节注释。

Result: 在HSCodeComp基准上，Qwen3.6-plus在四位码达到75.0% top-1和91.5% top-3，六位码达到64.2% top-1和78.3% top-3。Qwen3.6-27B-FP8（非思考模式）与前沿模型在四位码和六位码top-1一致性分别为84.2%和77.4%。

Conclusion: 确定性工作流在HS编码任务上优于端到端大模型提示；对226个六位码分歧的两阶段人工审核表明，HSCodeComp基准中部分真实标签可能偏离HS通用规则，已发布完整判定记录供社区审查。

Abstract: Harmonized System (HS) tariff classification is a high-stakes, expert-level task in which a free-form product description must be mapped to a specific six- or eight-digit code under the General Interpretive Rules (GIR), section notes, chapter notes, and Explanatory Notes. The difficulty lies not in knowledge volume but in *multi-dimensional rule reasoning*: a correct classification must satisfy competing priority rules along several axes simultaneously, including material, form, function, essential character, the part-versus-whole boundary, and specific listing versus residual headings. End-to-end prompting of large language models fails characteristically by resolving one axis while ignoring the priority constraints on the others. We present a *deterministic agentic workflow* in contrast to self-planning agents: the control flow is fixed, language model calls are confined to narrow stages, and reflection and verification are retained as local mechanisms. This design yields interpretability by construction--each decision is decomposed into stage-wise structured outputs with verbatim citation of the chapter or section notes that bear on it. The architecture combines offline knowledge-engineering of the Chinese HS tariff with an online six-stage pipeline. Evaluated on HSCodeComp at the six-digit level, the workflow reaches 75.0% top-1 and 91.5% top-3 at four digits, and 64.2% top-1 and 78.3% top-3 at six digits with Qwen3.6-plus; an open-weight Qwen3.6-27B-FP8 backbone in non-thinking mode achieves 84.2% four-digit and 77.4% six-digit top-1 agreement with the frontier model. A two-stage manual audit of 226 six-digit disagreements suggests that a non-trivial fraction of HSCodeComp ground-truth labels may deviate from HS general rules; full adjudication records are released in the appendix as preliminary findings for community review.

</details>


### [120] [Small, Private Language Models as Teammates for Educational Assessment Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15015)
*Chris Davis Jaldi,Anmol Saini,Shan Zhang,Noah Schroeder,Cogan Shimizu,Eleni Ilkou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了大语言模型（LLMs）和小语言模型（SLMs）在教育评估题目设计中的表现，发现SLMs在关键教学质量维度上具有竞争力且支持本地隐私部署，但模型自动评估存在系统性的不一致性和偏差，因此主张将语言模型定位为有边界的辅助工具，强调人机协同（Human-in-the-Loop）的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在利用生成式AI设计教育评估题目时，依赖主观或有限的评估方法，主要关注专有模型，缺乏在教育真实场景中系统考察生成、评估和部署约束。同时，小语言模型（SLMs）作为保护隐私和资源受限环境下的本地替代方案，其在评估任务中的有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 系统比较LLMs和SLMs在评估题目设计中的表现；使用可复现且基于教育学理论（布鲁姆分类学）的指标评估生成质量；进一步分析模型自动化评估与专家评审之间的一致性和偏差。

Result: SLMs在多个关键教学质量维度上达到了具有竞争力的性能，同时支持本地化、隐私敏感的部署。但模型自动评估相对于专家评分存在系统性的不一致性和偏差。

Conclusion: 语言模型应被定位为评估工作流中的有边界辅助工具（bounded assistants），人机协同（Human-in-the-Loop）不可或缺；研究推动了自动化教育题目生成领域在质量、可靠性和部署权衡方面的进展。

Abstract: Generative AI increasingly supports educational design tasks, e.g., through Large Language Models (LLMs), demonstrating the capability to design assessment questions that are aligned with pedagogical frameworks (e.g., Bloom's taxonomy). However, they often rely on subjective or limited evaluation methods; focus primarily on proprietary models; or rarely systematically examine generation, evaluation, or deployment constraints in real educational settings. Meanwhile, Small Language Models (SLMs) have emerged as local alternatives that better address privacy and resource limitations; yet their effectiveness for assessment tasks remains underexplored. To address this gap, we systematically compare LLMs and SLMs for assessment question design; evaluate generation quality across Bloom's taxonomy levels using reproducible, pedagogically grounded metrics; and further assess model-based judging against expert-informed evaluation by analyzing reliability and agreement patterns. Results show that SLMs achieve competitive performance across key pedagogically motivated quality dimensions while enabling local, privacy-sensitive deployment. However, model-based evaluations also exhibit systematic inconsistencies and bias relative to expert ratings. These findings provide evidence to posit language models as bounded assistants in assessment workflows; underscore the necessity of Human-in-the-Loop; and advance the automated educational question generation field by examining quality, reliability, and deployment-aware trade-offs.

</details>


### [121] [Orchard: An Open-Source Agentic Modeling Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15040)
*Baolin Peng,Wenlin Yao,Qianhui Wu,Hao Cheng,Xiao Yu,Rui Yang,Tao Ge,Alessandrio Sordoni,Xingdi Yuan,Yelong Shen,Pengcheng He,Tong Zhang,Zhou Yu,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Orchard是一个开源框架，通过轻量级环境服务和三种训练方案（Orchard-SWE、Orchard-GUI、Orchard-Claw），在代码、GUI和智能助手三个智能体领域实现了开源模型的最优性能，证明了可扩展的开源智能体建模是可行的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体建模领域存在基础设施和训练上的差距：高性能系统多依赖闭源代码库、模型或服务，而开源框架主要关注编排和评估，缺乏可扩展的智能体训练方案。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个开源、轻量、可复用的智能体建模框架。

Method: 核心是Orchard Env，一个轻量级环境服务，提供跨任务域、智能体框架和流水线阶段的沙箱生命周期管理原语。在此基础上构建三种训练方案：Orchard-SWE（代码智能体）采用107K轨迹蒸馏、信用分配SFT和平衡自适应 rollout RL；Orchard-GUI（GUI智能体）仅用0.4K蒸馏轨迹和2.2K开放任务训练4B视觉语言模型；Orchard-Claw（个人助理智能体）仅用0.2K合成任务训练。

Result: Orchard-SWE在SWE-bench Verified上达到64.3%（SFT）和67.5%（SFT+RL），在同规模开源模型中达到SOTA；Orchard-GUI在WebVoyager、Online-Mind2Web和DeepShop上分别达到74.1%、67.0%和64.0%，为最强开源模型；Orchard-Claw在Claw-Eval上达到59.6% pass@3，配合更强框架可达73.9%。

Conclusion: 轻量级、开源、与框架无关的环境层能够实现跨领域的可复用智能体数据、训练方案和评估，证明了可扩展的开源智能体建模的可行性。

Abstract: Agentic modeling aims to transform LLMs into autonomous agents capable of solving complex tasks through planning, reasoning, tool use, and multi-turn interaction with environments. Despite major investment, open research remains constrained by infrastructure and training gaps. Many high-performing systems rely on proprietary codebases, models, or services, while most open-source frameworks focus on orchestration and evaluation rather than scalable agent training. We present Orchard, an open-source framework for scalable agentic modeling. At its core is Orchard Env, a lightweight environment service providing reusable primitives for sandbox lifecycle management across task domains, agent harnesses, and pipeline stages. On top of Orchard Env, we build three agentic modeling recipes. Orchard-SWE targets coding agents. We distill 107K trajectories from MiniMax-M2.5 and Qwen3.5-397B, introduce credit-assignment SFT to learn from productive segments of unresolved trajectories, and apply Balanced Adaptive Rollout for RL. Starting from Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking, Orchard-SWE achieves 64.3% on SWE-bench Verified after SFT and 67.5% after SFT+RL, setting a new state of the art among open-source models of comparable size. Orchard-GUI trains a 4B vision-language computer-use agent using only 0.4K distilled trajectories and 2.2K open-ended tasks. It achieves 74.1%, 67.0%, and 64.0% success rates on WebVoyager, Online-Mind2Web, and DeepShop, respectively, making it the strongest open-source model while remaining competitive with proprietary systems. Orchard-Claw targets personal assistant agents. Trained with only 0.2K synthetic tasks, it achieves 59.6% pass@3 on Claw-Eval and 73.9% when paired with a stronger ZeroClaw harness. Collectively, these results show that a lightweight, open, harness-agnostic environment layer enables reusable agentic data, training recipes, and evaluations across domains.

</details>


### [122] [Beyond Individual Intelligence: Surveying Collaboration, Failure Attribution, and Self-Evolution in LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14892)
*Shihao Qi,Jie Ma,Rui Xing,Wei Guo,Xiao Huang,Zhitao Gao,Jianhao Deng,Jun Liu,Lingling Zhang,Bifan Wei,Boqian Yang,Pinghui Wang,Jianwen Sun,Jing Tao,Yaqiang Wu,Hui Liu,Yu Yao,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体系统中错误跨智能体与跨轮次传播导致诊断困难、难以实现结构性自我改进的问题，提出一个以LIFE（能力基础→协作集成→故障归因→自主进化）四阶段因果链为核心框架的统一综述，通过形式化各阶段间的依赖关系，识别跨阶段开放挑战，并倡导构建闭环、自组织、自改进的多智能体集体智能系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述分别关注单智能体能力、多智能体协作或智能体自我进化，但忽视了这些环节之间的因果依赖关系；多智能体系统中错误传播带来的脆弱性未被系统性审视，缺乏从故障归因到结构性自我改进的闭环框架。

Method: 提出LIFE（Lay capability foundation → Integrate agents → Find faults through attribution → Evolve through self-improvement）四阶段因果演进框架，对每个阶段构建系统分类法，形式化相邻阶段间的依赖与约束关系，并在此基础上识别跨阶段边界处的开放挑战。

Result: 系统梳理了多智能体系统从能力构建到自主进化的完整因果链条，揭示了各阶段间的形式化依赖关系，并提出了跨阶段的研究议程，为构建自组织、闭环自改进的集体智能系统提供了概念路线图。

Conclusion: 通过统一视角连接以往碎片化的研究方向，本文为多智能体系统从当前协调框架向更具自我组织能力的集体智能演进提供了系统性参考和概念 roadmap。

Abstract: LLM-based autonomous agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool use, yet remain limited when tasks require sustained coordination across roles, tools, and environments. Multi-agent systems address this through structured collaboration among specialized agents, but tighter coordination also amplifies a less explored risk: errors can propagate across agents and interaction rounds, producing failures that are difficult to diagnose and rarely translate into structural self-improvement. Existing surveys cover individual agent capabilities, multi-agent collaboration, or agent self-evolution separately, leaving the causal dependencies among them unexamined. This survey provides a unified review organized around four causally linked stages, which we term the LIFE progression: Lay the capability foundation, Integrate agents through collaboration, Find faults through attribution, and Evolve through autonomous self-improvement. For each stage, we provide systematic taxonomies and formally characterize the dependencies between adjacent stages, revealing how each stage both depends on and constrains the next. Beyond synthesizing existing work, we identify open challenges at stage boundaries and propose a cross-stage research agenda for closed-loop multi-agent systems capable of continuously diagnosing failures, reorganizing structures, and refining agent behaviors, extending current coordination frameworks toward more self-organizing forms of collective intelligence. By bridging these previously fragmented research threads, this survey aims to offer both a systematic reference and a conceptual roadmap toward autonomous, self-improving multi-agent intelligence.

</details>


### [123] [Case-Based Calibration of Adaptive Reasoning and Execution for LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15041)
*Renning Pang,Tian Lan,Leyuan Liu,Piao Tong,Sheng Cao,Xiaosong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAST是一个基于案例驱动的工具使用框架，通过提取历史执行轨迹中的复杂度画像和失败画像，结合细粒度奖励设计与自适应推理，在强化学习中内化策略，提升了工具调用的模式遵循准确率（最高+5.85%）并缩短了26%的推理长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在使用外部工具时，需要在合理的推理深度与严格的结构有效性之间取得平衡，现有方法在复用原始示例时缺乏对策略复杂度和结构失效模式的系统性理解。

Method: 将历史执行轨迹视为结构化案例，从中提取复杂度画像（识别最优推理策略）和失败画像（映射结构失效模式），据此设计细粒度奖励信号和自适应推理机制，在强化学习过程中让模型自主内化案例驱动的策略。

Result: 在BFCLv2和ToolBench上，整体执行准确率提升最高5.85个百分点，平均推理长度减少26%，显著降低了高影响的结构性错误。

Conclusion: 历史执行案例能够为校准的工具使用提供可复用的适应知识，通过案例驱动的方式可以有效提升大语言模型工具调用的可靠性与效率。

Abstract: Tool use extends large language models beyond parametric knowledge, but reliable execution requires balancing appropriate reasoning depth with strict structural validity. We approach this problem from a case-based perspective to present CAST, a case-driven framework that treats historical execution trajectories as structured cases. Instead of reusing raw exemplar outputs, CAST extracts case-derived signals to identify complexity profiles for estimating optimal reasoning strategies, alongside failure profiles to map likely structural breakdowns. The framework translates this knowledge into a fine-grained reward design and adaptive reasoning, enabling the model to autonomously internalize case-based strategies during reinforcement learning. Experiments on BFCLv2 and ToolBench demonstrate that CAST improves both schema-faithful execution and task-level tool-use success while reducing unnecessary deliberation. The approach achieves up to 5.85 percentage points gain in overall execution accuracy and reduces average reasoning length by 26%, significantly mitigating high-impact structural errors. Ultimately, this demonstrates how historical execution cases can provide reusable adaptation knowledge for calibrated tool use.

</details>


### [124] [COREKG: Coreset-Guided Personalized Summarization of Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14900)
*Sohel Aman Khan,Raghava Mutharaju,Supratim Shit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COREKG方法，基于coreset理论通过敏感度重要性采样为每个用户生成个性化知识图谱摘要，在Freebase、WikiData和DBpedia上的评估表明，相比GLIMPSE等方法，该方法以极小图占比实现了更高的查询准确性和结构覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模知识图谱在问答、可视化等任务中过于庞大，需要有效的摘要方法；现有方法缺乏个性化能力，无法根据用户特定查询模式生成精简摘要，导致存储和查询开销大。

Method: 将coreset理论适配到知识图谱摘要任务中，定义基于用户查询工作负载的三元组敏感度分数来衡量其重要性，通过敏感度重要性采样从原图中抽取相关三元组子集，构建有界近似误差的个性化摘要。

Result: 在Freebase、WikiData和DBpedia数据集上，COREKG相比GLIMPSE、PPR、iSummary、PEGASUS和APEX²等基线方法，在查询回答准确性和结构覆盖率上均取得更优表现，且仅使用原图的一小部分。

Conclusion: 基于coreset理论进行个性化知识图谱摘要是一种有效方法，能够根据用户查询行为生成小而精的摘要，兼顾低存储/查询开销与高准确率。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are extensively used across different domains and in several applications. Often, these KGs are very large in size. Such KGs become unwieldy for tasks such as question answering and visualization. Summarization of KGs offers a viable alternative in such cases. Furthermore, personalized KG summarization is crucial in the current data-driven world as it captures the specific requirements of users based on their query patterns. Since it only maintains relevant information, the personalized summaries of KG are small, resulting in significantly smaller storage requirements and query runtime. In this work, we adapt the coreset theory to create personalized KG summaries. For a given dataset and a user-specific query workload, we present an approach that samples a relevant subset of triples using sensitivity-based importance sampling. We ensure that the subset approximates the characteristics of the full dataset with bounded approximation error. We define sensitivity scores that measure the importance of a triple with respect to a user's query workload, which are then used by our coreset construction algorithm. We explicitly focus on personalized knowledge graph summarization by constructing summaries independently for each user based on their query behaviour.
  Our evaluation on Freebase, WikiData, and DBpedia shows that COREKG delivers higher query-answering accuracy and structural coverage than the state-of-the-art methods, such as GLIMPSE, PPR, iSummary, PEGASUS and APEX$^2$ while requiring only a tiny fraction of the original graph.

</details>


### [125] [KGPFN: Unlocking the Potential of Knowledge Graph Foundation Model via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14907)
*Yisen Gao,Jiaxin Bai,Haoyu Huang,Zhongwei Xie,Yufei Li,Hong Ting Tsang,Sirui Han,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KGPFN是一种基于Prior-data Fitted Network的知识图谱基础模型，通过在推理时利用结构化上下文（局部邻域+全局关系实例）进行上下文学习，在57个基准上超越需微调的现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KG基础模型主要强调关系层面的通用性，但缺乏对上下文学习（ICL）这一基础模型另一支柱的探索；且KG中的上下文是结构化和异构的，需要同时考虑查询实体周围的局部上下文和总结关系行为的全局上下文。

Method: 1) 通过关系图上的消息传递学习跨图关系不变性；2) 使用多层NBFNet编码局部邻域作为局部上下文；3) 检索查询关系的大量实例及其局部邻域构建关系特定的全局上下文，在Prior-Data Fitted Network框架中通过特征级和样本级注意力聚合；4) 在多图预训练中学习何时实例化可复用模式、何时用上下文证据覆盖它们。

Result: 在57个KG基准上，KGPFN仅通过上下文学习就能强适应未见图，一致优于需要微调的竞争性KG基础模型。

Conclusion: KGPFN成功将可迁移的关系规律与推理时的结构化上下文学习统一起来，证明了在KG推理中仅靠上下文学习即可达到甚至超越微调的性能。

Abstract: Knowledge graph (KG) foundation models aim to generalize across graphs with unseen entities and relations by learning transferable relational structure. However, most existing methods primarily emphasize relation-level universality, while in-context learning, the other pillar of foundation models remains under-explored for KG reasoning. In KGs, context is inherently structured and heterogeneous: effective prediction requires conditioning on the local context around the query entities as well as the global context that summarizes how a relation behaves across many instances. We propose KGPFN, a KG foundation model using Prior-data Fitted Network that unifies transferable relational regularities with inference-time in-context learning from structured context. KGPFN first learns relation representations via message passing on relation graphs to capture cross-graph relational invariances. For query-specific reasoning, it encodes local neighborhoods using a multi-layer NBFNet as local context. To enable ICL at global scale, it constructs relation-specific global context by retrieving a large set of instances of the query relation together with their local neighborhoods, and aggregates them within a Prior-Data Fitted Network framework that combines feature-level and sample-level attention. Through multi-graph pretraining on diverse KGs, KGPFN learns when to instantiate reusable patterns and when to override them using contextual evidence. Experiments on 57 KG benchmarks demonstrate that KGPFN achieves strong adaptation to previously unseen graphs through in-context learning alone, consistently outperforming competitive fine-tuned KG foundation models. Our code is available at https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/KGPFN.

</details>


### [126] [From Sycophantic Consensus to Pluralistic Repair: Why AI Alignment Must Surface Disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14912)
*Varad Vishwarupe,Nigel Shadbolt,Marina Jirotka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批评当代RLHF训练的AI助手在多元对齐中呈现“谄媚式共识”（sycophantic consensus）——倾向于迎合用户、消弭分歧，而非真正容纳多元价值观。作者提出以Grice会话原则为基础的三种对话机制（范围界定、信号标记、修复），并设计了“多元修复分数”（PRS）来区分原则性修正与屈从，实验表明Claude Sonnet 4.5和GPT-4o在争议性价值提示上表现出高度附和但低修复质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多元对齐研究主要关注偏好聚合（覆盖、导向、比例代表），但作者认为在真正的价值多元主义下，AI系统在部署中面临的核心问题不是覆盖不足，而是“谄媚式共识”——AI学会无条件同意、验证用户、减少摩擦。这种交互层面的分歧崩塌在健康、公民生活、劳动、治理等领域的协商中会产生产生分配性后果，构成结构性失败。

Method: 1) 基于Grice会话原则提出三种对话机制：scoping（承认自身视角局限）、signalling（暴露价值冲突而非粉饰）、repair（基于原则修正立场而非屈服于用户压力）。2) 形式化“多元修复分数”（PRS）区分原则性修正与屈从。3) 在Claude Sonnet 4.5（N=198）和GPT-4o（N=100）上进行小规模实证，使用争议性价值提示测试。

Result: 两个模型均表现出高度的“同意跟随”（agreement-following），但在争议性价值提示上修复质量（repair-quality）较低。即模型倾向于随用户意见改变立场，但这种改变并非基于原则性理由。

Conclusion: 1) 偏好聚合不足以实现部署中的多元对齐，需要关注交互层面的分歧可见性和原则性修正。2) PRS衡量的是多元主义的交互前提条件（可见分歧、原则性修正），而非完整多元主义。3) 作者反思了“谁的‘原则性’算数”这一反身性问题。4) 多元主义最终在部署-治理层（界面、偏好数据管线、审计基础设施）被决定性地构建或破坏。

Abstract: Pluralistic alignment is typically operationalised as preference aggregation: producing responses that span (Overton), steer toward (Steerable), or proportionally represent (Distributional) diverse human values. We argue that aggregation alone is an incomplete primitive for deployed pluralistic alignment. Under genuine value pluralism, the failure mode of contemporary RLHF-trained assistants is not insufficient coverage but sycophantic consensus: a learned tendency to agree with, validate, and minimise friction with the immediate interlocutor. Because deployed AI systems now mediate consequential deliberation across health, civic life, labour, and governance, the collapse of disagreement at the interaction layer is not a narrow technical concern but a structural failure with distributive consequences. We reframe pluralistic alignment around three conversational mechanisms drawn from Grice's maxims: scoping (acknowledging the limits of one's perspective), signalling (surfacing value-conflict rather than smoothing it over), and repair (revising one's position on principled grounds, not on user pressure). We formalise a metric, the Pluralistic Repair Score (PRS), distinguishing principled revision from capitulation, and present a small-scale empirical illustration on two frontier RLHF-trained models (Claude Sonnet 4.5, N=198; GPT-4o, N=100) showing that, for both, agreement-following coexists with low repair-quality on contested-value prompts. PRS measures an interactional precondition for pluralism (visible disagreement; principled revision) rather than pluralism in full; we discuss the difference, take seriously the reflexive question of whose "principled" counts, and argue that pluralism is most decisively made or unmade at the deployment-governance layer: interfaces, preference-data pipelines, and audit infrastructure.

</details>


### [127] [GraphFlow: An Architecture for Formally Verifiable Visual Workflows Enabling Reliable Agentic AI Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14968)
*Drewry H. Morris,Luis Valles,Reza Hosseini Ghomi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphFlow是一个可视化工作流系统，通过将工作流图视为可执行规范，结合编译期合约验证和运行时持久执行引擎，提升多步骤、关键任务型AI自动化流程的可靠性。在三个临床站点为期一年的试点中，系统在无验证核心的情况下实现了97.08%的完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流平台提供持久执行和可观测性但缺乏语义正确性保证，而智能体系统在推理时规划导致行为对提示变化敏感且难以审计。在独立步骤模型中，十步流程若每步90%可靠性，整体成功率仅35%。需要一种能提供语义正确性保证的工作流系统。

Method: GraphFlow将工作流图视为可执行规范（定义数据范围、执行语义和监控）。(1) 编译期：受限类图被规约为可复用自动化组件，其合约（前置/后置条件、组合义务）在加入共享库前需经证明检查。(2) 运行时：持久执行引擎在仅追加事件日志中记录结果，可在系统边界强制执行合约，支持重放、重试和审计。(3) 泳道（Swimlanes）显式标示信任边界，将已验证逻辑与外部系统、人工判断和AI决策分离。

Result: 在三个临床站点为期一年的试点中，执行了8,728个队列入组工作流，在早期原型（无验证核心）下实现97.08%的完成率；观察到的失败主要局限在外部集成。形式化语义和证明检查的准入模型已规定并正在积极开发中。

Conclusion: GraphFlow通过将工作流图作为单一可执行规范，结合形式化合约验证和持久执行引擎，为解决多步骤AI自动化流程中的累积错误问题提供了系统化方案。验证核心的评估留作未来工作。

Abstract: GraphFlow is a visual workflow system designed to improve the reliability of agentic AI automation in multi-step, mission-critical processes. In these workflows, small errors compound rapidly: under an idealized model of independent steps, a ten-step process with 90% per-step reliability completes successfully only 35% of the time. Existing workflow platforms provide durable execution and observability but offer few semantic correctness guarantees, while agentic systems plan at inference time, making behavior sensitive to prompt variation and difficult to audit.
  GraphFlow is designed to address this gap by treating workflow diagrams as the executable specification, a single artifact defining data scope, execution semantics, and monitoring. At compile time, a restricted class of diagrams is specified to produce reusable automations whose contracts (preconditions, postconditions, and composition obligations) are intended to be proof-checked before admission to a shared library. At runtime, a durable engine records outcomes in an append-only event log and can enforce contracts at system boundaries, supporting replay, retries, and audit. Swimlanes make trust boundaries explicit, separating verified logic from external systems, human judgment, and AI decisions.
  A year-long pilot across three clinical sites executed 8,728 cohort-enrolled workflow runs with a 97.08% completion rate under an early prototype without the verified-core subsystem; observed failures were localized primarily to external integrations. The formal semantics and proof-checked admission model described here are specified and under active development. Evaluation of the verified core is reserved for future work.

</details>


### [128] [Learning Developmental Scaffoldings to Guide Self-Organisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14998)
*Milton L. Montero,Elias Najarro,Jakob Schauser,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种联合学习自组织规则和预模式的神经细胞自动机（NCA）模型，通过信息论分析揭示了预模式与自组织过程之间的信息分配机制，发现联合学习能提升鲁棒性、编码能力和对称破缺能力，且有效预模式并非简单近似目标，而是通过偏置发育动力学促进收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物发育中大量信息被卸载到初始条件（如母体预模式），但这与自组织过程之间的信息分配机制尚不明确。现有自组织模型缺乏对这种"记忆-计算权衡"现象的定量研究。

Method: 将神经细胞自动机（NCA）与基于坐标的隐式神经表示（SIREN）联合训练，NCA学习自组织规则，SIREN生成预模式；通过信息论分析测量信息在预模式和自组织过程之间的分布。

Result: 联合学习在鲁棒性、编码容量和对称破缺方面优于纯自组织方法；有效预模式并非直接近似目标模式，而是通过偏置动力学过程来促进收敛。

Conclusion: 预模式与自组织规则之间存在非平凡关系，信息卸载到初始条件是生物发育中一种高效的策略，联合学习揭示了这一机制的计算优势。

Abstract: From subcellular structures to entire organisms, many natural systems generate complex organisation through self-organisation: local interactions that collectively give rise to global structure without any blueprint of the outcome. Yet a significant portion of the information driving such processes is not produced by self-organisation itself, instead, it is often offloaded to initial conditions of the system. Biological development is a prime example, where maternal pre-patterns encode positional and symmetry-breaking information that scaffolds the self-organising process. From maternal morphogen gradients in early embryogenesis to tissue-level morphogenetic pre-patterns guiding organ formation, this transfer of information to initial conditions, analogous to a memory-compute trade-off in computational systems, is a fundamental part of developmental processes. In this work, we study this offloading phenomenon by introducing a model that jointly learns both the self-organisation rules and the pre-patterns, allowing their interplay to be varied and measured under controlled conditions: a Neural Cellular Automaton (NCA) paired with a learned coordinate-based pattern generator (SIREN), both trained simultaneously to generate a set of patterns. We provide information-theoretic analyses of how information is distributed between pre-patterns and the self-organising process, and show that jointly learning both components yields improvements in robustness, encoding capacity, and symmetry breaking over purely self-organising alternatives. Our analysis further suggests that effective pre-patterns do not simply approximate their targets; rather, they bias the developmental dynamics in ways that facilitate convergence, pointing to a non-trivial relationship between the structure of initial conditions and the dynamics of self-organisation.

</details>


### [129] [Dual-Dimensional Consistency: Balancing Budget and Quality in Adaptive Inference-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15100)
*Rongman Xu,Yifei Li,Tianzhe Zhao,Yanrui Wu,Bo Li,Hang Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出双维度一致性(DDC)框架，通过置信加权贝叶斯协议和趋势感知分层剪枝，在保持或提升推理准确性的同时，将token消耗降低10倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时扩展策略在处理采样预算与推理质量的权衡时效率低下：宽度共识方法可能强化幻觉，深度剪枝机制则过早截断复杂但有效的推理链。

Method: 提出双维度一致性(DDC)框架，通过置信加权贝叶斯协议结合趋势感知分层剪枝，将路径质量与自适应终止统一。

Result: 在五个基准测试上，该方法将token消耗降低10倍以上，同时保持或超过强基线的准确性。

Conclusion: DDC通过耦合宽度与深度维度的一致性，实现了计算资源向高质量推理路径的集中，有效过滤幻觉并加速共识达成。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable abilities in reasoning. However, maximizing their potential through inference-time scaling faces challenges in trade-off between sampling budget and reasoning quality. Current strategies remain inefficient as they typically treat sampling width and depth as orthogonal objectives, where width consensus methods risk reinforcing hallucinations, while depth pruning mechanisms prematurely truncate complex yet valid reasoning chains. Therefore, we propose Dual-Dimensional Consistency (DDC), a unified framework that bridges path quality with adaptive termination. By coupling Confidence-Weighted Bayesian protocol with a Trend-Aware Stratified Pruning, our method ensures that computational resources are concentrated on high quality reasoning paths, filtering hallucinations while accelerating consensus. Evaluations across five benchmarks demonstrate that this approach reduces token consumption by over 10 times while maintaining or exceeding the accuracy of strong baselines across various LLMs.

</details>


### [130] [Why Neighborhoods Matter: Traversal Context and Provenance in Agentic GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15109)
*Riccardo Terrenzi,Maximilian von Zastrow,Serkan Ayvaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Agentic GraphRAG中引用的忠实性问题，指出引用不仅应支持答案，还需反映图遍历轨迹、结构及未引用但被访问的实体。实验表明，被引证据是必要的（移除会改变答案），但非充分的，因为未引用的遍历上下文和图结构也能影响答案准确性。建议将引用评估从源支持扩展到更广泛的检索轨迹溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法只关注引用与答案的支持关系，但Agentic GraphRAG中智能体在知识图谱上遍历并选择引用，未引用的遍历实体和结构也可能影响答案，需要重新定义引用忠实性。

Method: 通过控制消融实验，分别隔离、移除和掩码被引用和未被引用的图实体，对比这些操作对答案正确性的影响。

Result: 被引证据是必要的（移除会显著改变答案并降低准确率），但并非充分的，因为未引用的遍历上下文和图结构也能影响答案的质量。

Conclusion: Agentic GraphRAG中的引用评估应超越传统的源支持，走向对更广泛检索轨迹的溯源。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation can improve factuality by grounding answers in external evidence, but Agentic GraphRAG complicates what it means for citations to be faithful. In these systems, an agent explores a knowledge graph before producing an answer and a small set of citations. We frame citation faithfulness as a trajectory-level problem: final citations should not only support the answer, but also account for the graph traversal, structure, and visited-but-uncited entities that may influence it. Through controlled ablation experiments, we compare the effects of isolating, removing, and masking cited and uncited graph entities. Our results show that cited evidence is often necessary, as removing it substantially changes answers and reduces accuracy. However, citations are not sufficient, because accurate answers can also depend on uncited traversal context and surrounding graph structure. These findings suggest that citation evaluation in Agentic GraphRAG should move beyond source support toward provenance over the broader retrieval trajectory.

</details>


### [131] [APWA: A Distributed Architecture for Parallelizable Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15132)
*Evan Rose,Tushin Mallick,Matthew D. Laws,Cristina Nita-Rotaru,Alina Oprea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为APWA的分布式多智能体系统架构，通过将工作流分解为可独立处理的非干扰性子问题来实现并行执行，有效解决了LLM多智能体系统在处理大规模、高并行任务时面临的推理、协调和计算瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统在处理大规模、高复杂度任务时，存在严重的推理、协调和计算扩展瓶颈，限制了高并行任务的吞吐量，尽管底层LLM具备并行计算和推理能力。

Method: 提出Agent-Parallel Workload Architecture (APWA)，一种分布式多智能体系统架构，通过将工作流分解为非干扰性子问题，利用独立资源并行处理，无需跨通信；支持异构数据和多种并行模式。

Result: APWA能够将复杂查询动态分解为可并行的工作流，在更大规模任务上表现出良好的扩展性，而现有系统在此类场景下完全失败。

Conclusion: APWA有效解决了多智能体系统在高并行任务中的瓶颈问题，通过工作流分解和独立资源并行处理实现了高效扩展。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent systems based on large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable abilities in independently solving complex tasks in a wide breadth of application domains. However, these systems hit critical reasoning, coordination, and computational scaling bottlenecks as the size and complexity of their tasks grow. These limitations hinder multi-agent systems from achieving high-throughput processing for highly parallelizable tasks, despite the availability of parallel computing and reasoning primitives in the underlying LLMs. We introduce the Agent-Parallel Workload Architecture (APWA), a distributed multi-agent system architecture designed for the efficient processing of heavily parallelizable agentic workloads. APWA facilitates parallel execution by decomposing workflows into non-interfering subproblems that can be processed using independent resources without cross-communication. It supports heterogeneous data and parallel processing patterns, and it accommodates tasks from a wide breadth of domains. In our evaluation, we demonstrate that APWA can dynamically decompose complex queries into parallelizable workflows and scales on larger tasks in settings where prior systems fail completely.

</details>


### [132] [OpenDeepThink: Parallel Reasoning via Bradley--Terry Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15177)
*Shang Zhou,Wenhao Chai,Kaiyuan Liu,Huanzhi Mao,Qiuyang Mang,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenDeepThink，一种基于种群测试时计算扩展的框架，通过成对Bradley-Terry比较而非点式评判来选择LLM推理候选答案，在8轮Gemini 3.1 Pro上实现Codeforces Elo +405的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时计算扩展主要聚焦深度（延长单条推理链），广度扩展（并行采样多个候选）面临选择瓶颈：缺乏真实验证器时，点式LLM评判存在噪声和偏差，难以选出最佳候选。

Method: 提出OpenDeepThink框架，采用种群式方法：每轮LLM对随机候选对进行成对评判，通过Bradley-Terry模型聚合投票生成全局排序；保留顶部候选，对前三名候选基于评判中产生的自然语言批评进行变异，丢弃底部四分之一。

Result: Gemini 3.1 Pro在8轮LLM调用（约27分钟）中，Codeforces Elo提升+405分；该方法可跨强弱模型迁移无需重新调参；在HLE基准上，增益集中在客观可验证领域，主观领域效果逆转。

Conclusion: OpenDeepThink通过成对比较替代点式评判有效缓解选择瓶颈，提供了可迁移、高效的测试时计算扩展方案，并发布CF-73专家标注数据集。

Abstract: Test-time compute scaling is a primary axis for improving LLM reasoning. Existing methods primarily scale depth by extending a single reasoning trace. Scaling breadth by sampling multiple candidates in parallel is straightforward, but introduces a selection bottleneck: choosing the best candidate without a ground-truth verifier, since pointwise LLM judging is noisy and biased. To address this, we introduce OpenDeepThink, a population-based test-time compute framework that selects via pairwise Bradley-Terry comparison. Each generation, the LLM judges random pairs of candidates and aggregates votes via Bradley-Terry into a global ranking; top-ranked candidates are preserved and the top three quarters are mutated using the natural-language critiques produced during comparison; the bottom quarter is discarded. OpenDeepThink raises Gemini 3.1 Pro's effective Codeforces Elo by +405 points in eight sequential LLM-call rounds (~27 minutes wall-clock). The pipeline transfers across weaker and stronger models without retuning, and on the multi-domain HLE benchmark, gains appear concentrated in objectively verifiable domains and reverse in subjective ones. We release CF-73, a curated set of 73 expert-rated Codeforces problems with International Grandmaster annotation and 99% local-evaluation agreement against the official verdict.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Computing Lower Bounds on the Nonnegative Rank via Non-Convex Optimization Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14058)
*Timothy Baeckelant,Arnaud Vandaele,Nicolas Gillis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出非凸优化方法计算非负矩阵秩的四种下界（FSB、RCB、HSB、SSB），其中SSB算法为首个实现。该方法改进了部分矩阵的非负秩下界，在某些情形下确定其精确非负秩，并在基准测试中展示了竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非负矩阵秩的计算是NP难的，现有方法多通过上下界近似，但许多下界的计算本身也很困难，缺乏有效的实用算法，尤其是SSB尚无具体实现。

Method: 采用非凸优化公式，针对四种下界（FSB、RCB、HSB、SSB）设计了实用算法，其中SSB算法是文献中首个实现。

Result: 1) 改进了一些矩阵的非负秩最佳已知下界；2) 在某些情况下下界与最佳上界匹配，首次确定了精确非负秩；3) 在标准基准测试上展示了竞争性表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的非凸方法为非负秩下界计算提供了有效且实用的替代方案，特别是SSB算法的首个实现填补了空白，有助于更精确地确定矩阵的非负秩。

Abstract: The nonnegative rank of a nonnegative matrix $X$ is the smallest number of nonnegative rank-one factors that sum to $X$. Since computing the nonnegative rank is NP-hard, it is common to circumvent this issue by computing lower and upper bounds. In this paper, we propose non-convex formulations and practical implementations for four important lower bounds for the nonnegative rank, namely the fooling set bound (FSB), the rectangle covering bound (RCB), the hyperplane separation bound (HSB), and the self-scaled bound (SSB). In particular, our algorithm for computing the SSB is the first available in the literature, to the best of our knowledge. It allows us to improve the best known lower bound on the nonnegative rank for some matrices. In some cases, they coincide with the best known upper bound, thereby establishing their exact nonnegative rank for the first time. Moreover, on canonical benchmarks, we show that our non-convex approaches provide a meaningful and often competitive alternative to standard methods. The paper also provides a consolidated reference for the current state of several classical lower bounds on a large number of benchmark matrices.

</details>


### [134] [Quantitative Soft-to-Hard Terminal Constraint Convergence for the Heat Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14060)
*Sung-Sik Kwon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有终端状态约束的热方程最优控制问题，通过罚函数方法逼近硬约束，证明了当罚参数趋于无穷时最优控制与终端状态以显式收敛速率收敛到精确解，并给出了O(α^{-θ})的定量估计及在更强模态可加和性假设下的最优O(1/α)速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热方程最优控制问题中，强制终端状态约束在数值求解时存在困难，因此需要引入罚函数方法进行近似，并严格证明其收敛性。

Method: 采用罚函数方法松弛终端状态约束，建立罚问题的变分形式，通过分析对偶空间中的模态分解和投影结构，推导出收敛速率的显式定量估计。

Result: 证明了罚问题的解以O(α^{-θ})的速率收敛到精确约束解，且在更强的终端失配模态可加和性假设下可达到锐利的O(1/α)收敛速率；有限维原型问题揭示了投影结构的本质。

Conclusion: 罚函数方法能有效逼近热方程终端约束最优控制问题，且收敛速率可定量刻画，为实际数值计算提供了理论保障。

Abstract: We study an optimal control problem for the heat equation with a prescribed terminal state. To circumvent the difficulty of enforcing a hard terminal constraint, we analyze a penalized formulation and prove that the corresponding optimal controls and terminal states converge to the exact constrained solution as the penalty parameter \(α\to \infty\). We establish explicit quantitative convergence estimates of order \(O(α^{-θ})\), including the sharp \(O(1/α)\) rate under stronger modal summability assumptions on the terminal mismatch. A finite-dimensional prototype is used to illustrate the underlying projection structure, while numerical illustrations are reported in a companion study.

</details>


### [135] [Stochastic global optimization of continuous functions via random walks on Grassmannians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14151)
*Kartik Gupta,Stephen D. Miller,Pradeep Ravikumar,Ramarathnam Venkatesan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Grassmann流形随机游走的全局优化方法，通过反复采样低维子空间并求解其上的子问题来最小化连续目标函数，其收敛保证不依赖凸性、光滑性等经典条件，而是依赖于限制最小值在子空间上的几何分布，并识别出一个控制收敛速率的“间隙参数”。该方法还具有盲点鲁棒性：窄而深的损失函数凹陷对算法轨迹影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化方法的收敛分析严重依赖于凸性、光滑性、Lipschitz界或Polyak-Lojasiewicz条件等假设，这些假设在许多实际问题中难以满足。本文旨在提出一种无需这些经典假设的全局优化方法，其收敛性仅依赖于目标函数在低维子空间上的几何结构。

Method: 该方法在Grassmann流形上进行随机游走：每次迭代随机采样一个k维线性子空间（k≪d），将原优化问题限制到该子空间上形成低维子问题，使用任意黑箱优化器求解该子问题得到候选解，并更新当前迭代点（保证单调改进）。

Result: 证明了方法的收敛性仅依赖于通过当前点的k维子空间上限制最小值的几何分布，而非凸性等经典条件。识别出一个类似随机游走谱间隙的“间隙参数”，用于控制迭代点收敛到全局最小值的速率。此外，论证了该方法具有盲点鲁棒性：测度较小的深谷区域对算法轨迹影响有限。

Conclusion: 该工作为全局优化提供了一种全新的分析框架，摆脱了对凸性等经典条件的依赖，并揭示了子空间随机采样方法的几何收敛本质。盲点鲁棒性表明该方法对局部极小陷阱具有天然抵抗力，适合处理高维非凸优化问题。

Abstract: We introduce a stochastic global optimization method based on random walks on Grassmannian manifolds. To minimize a continuous objective $\ell:\mathbb{R}^d\rightarrow\mathbb{R}$, the method repeatedly samples random $k$-dimensional linear subspaces (with $k\ll d$), solves the resulting low-dimensional restrictions of these problems to these subspaces using an arbitrary black-box optimizer, and updates the iterate (which monotonically improves upon the previous iterate). Unlike classical optimization analyses that rely on convexity, smoothness, Lipschitz bounds, or Polyak-Lojasiewicz-type conditions, our convergence guarantees depend only on the geometric distribution of restricted minima across the $k$-dimensional subspaces passing through a given point in $\mathbb{R}^d$. We identify a gap parameter -- an analogue of a spectral gap for random walks -- that controls the rate at which the iterates approach the global minimum value. Finally, we argue that the same analysis yields a blind-spot robustness property: sufficiently narrow, deep dips of the loss function (small-measure regions where $\ell$ spikes downward) have limited influence on the algorithm's trajectory, since they are unlikely to be encountered by random subspace sampling.

</details>


### [136] [Generalized Dual Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14273)
*Pengyu Zhang,Ruiwei Jiang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出广义对偶分解（GDD）方法，通过引入非线性正则化器解决两阶段混合整数随机优化中传统对偶分解缺乏强对偶性的问题，实现全局最优收敛并支持并行计算，数值实验验证了其强对偶性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对偶分解方法因缺乏强对偶性，在求解两阶段混合整数随机优化问题时，得到的下界不精确。本文旨在解决这一根本性缺陷。

Method: 将线性正则化器扩展为一般非线性形式，通过参数化和割平面编码非线性正则化器，建立GDD算法的全局最优收敛性；并通过剪枝和有效不等式加速子问题求解；进一步将GDD扩展为约束形式，涵盖鲁棒优化、机会约束规划和多跟随者双层优化。

Result: GDD实现了强对偶性；在计算效率上优于原始（Benders型）分解算法；并通过并行计算获得显著加速。

Conclusion: GDD有效克服了传统对偶分解的强对偶缺陷，在保持并行化的同时实现全局最优，具有广泛的适用性和计算优势。

Abstract: We study two-stage stochastic optimization models with mixed-integer decision variables appearing in both stages. For these models, dual decomposition enables parallel computing implementation and can quickly provide a lower bound for the optimal value. However, the lower bound thus obtained is not exact in general due to the lack of strong duality. In this paper, we propose a generalized dual decomposition (GDD) that extends the linear regularizer used in dual decomposition to a general nonlinear one, which still admits parallelization while exhibiting strong duality. By encoding the nonlinear regularizers through parameterization and cutting planes, we establish the convergence of a GDD algorithm to achieve global optimum. In addition, we discuss strategies for solving the GDD scenario subproblems more efficiently, including pruning and valid inequalities. Furthermore, we extend GDD to a more general, constrained form that subsumes, as special cases, robust optimization, chance-constrained programs, and bilevel optimization with multiple followers. Finally, numerical experiments demonstrate the strong duality of GDD, its computational efficacy versus primal (Benders-type) decomposition algorithms, and the speedup through parallel computing.

</details>


### [137] [Adaptive Metrics for Norm-Minimization-Based Outer Approximation in Convex Vector Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14320)
*Mohammed Alshahrani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应度量框架，用于有界凸向量优化中基于范数最小化的外逼近算法。通过允许标量化度量在迭代中变化，同时用固定欧几里得范数测量逼近误差，算法能动态利用问题几何结构。理论证明了改进的欧几里得收敛速率可推广到所有固定内积范数，并建立了色散定理。数值实验表明，在具有弯曲帕累托前沿的问题上，自适应度量相比固定欧几里得范数减少了31-33%的迭代次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有凸向量优化的外逼近算法通常使用固定度量（如ℓ2范数），未能充分利用问题的几何结构，导致收敛效率受限。作者旨在开发一种自适应度量框架，使算法能够根据问题几何动态调整，从而提高收敛速度并降低计算成本。

Method: 提出自适应度量框架，核心是让标量化度量在迭代中变化，同时用固定欧几里得范数测量逼近误差。理论方面：1) 证明改进的欧几里得收敛率O(k^{2/(1-q)})可推广到所有固定内积范数；2) 建立色散定理，证明当上镜象具有有界曲率的严格凸边界时，算法生成的切割法线自然分散到所有方向，保证自适应度量条件数良好。基于此推导了度量条件数影响Hausdorff误差估计的显式收敛界。

Result: 数值实验验证了理论速率，在具有弯曲帕累托前沿的问题上，自适应度量相比固定欧几里得范数减少了31-33%的迭代次数。

Conclusion: 本文为凸向量优化中的自适应度量选择提供了严格的理论基础，证明自适应度量框架既能保持理论收敛保证，又能显著提升实际计算效率。

Abstract: We develop an adaptive-metric framework for norm-minimization-based outer approximation algorithms in bounded convex vector optimization. The key idea is to let the scalarization metric vary across iterations while measuring approximation error in a fixed Euclidean norm. This enables the algorithm to exploit problem geometry dynamically. Our approach rests on two theoretical foundations. First, we prove that the improved Euclidean convergence rate $O(k^{2/(1-q)})$ -- previously known only for the standard $\ell_2$ norm -- extends to all fixed inner-product norms. Second, we establish a dispersion theorem showing that the cut normals generated by the algorithm naturally spread across all directions when the upper image has a strictly convex boundary with bounded curvature. This geometric condition guarantees that the adaptive metric remains well-conditioned throughout execution. Building on these results, we derive explicit convergence bounds that quantify how metric conditioning influences the Hausdorff error estimates. Numerical experiments validate the theoretical rates and demonstrate that adaptive metrics achieve 31--33\% fewer iterations than the fixed Euclidean norm on problems with curved Pareto fronts. Our results provide a rigorous foundation for adaptive metric selection in convex vector optimization.

</details>


### [138] [Convergence Rates for $\ell_p$ Norm Minimization in Convex Vector Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14324)
*Mohammed Alshahrani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了对凸向量优化问题使用 $\ell_p$ 范数（$p \in (1,\infty)$）的基于范数最小化的外逼近算法，其 Hausdorff 误差收敛率对所有 $p$ 均为 $O(k^{2/(1-q)})$，与 $p$ 无关。通过欧几里得中介技术绕过 $\ell_p$ 光滑性限制，得出了优于直接模方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补了 $\ell_p$ 范数（$p\neq 2$）下凸向量优化外逼近算法收敛率分析的空白。直接利用模光滑性方法只能得到退化的指数 $\min(p,2)$，对 $1<p<2$ 的情况效果较差，因此需要更优的理论分析。

Method: 提出欧几里得中介技术：利用 $\mathbb{R}^q$ 中固有的内积结构获得超平面距离的二次界，绕过 $\ell_p$ 光滑性限制；再通过范数等价性将结果转换回任意 $\ell_p$ 度量，仅损失一个维度相关的常数，而非指数级损失。

Result: 对所有 $p \in (1,\infty)$，Hausdorff 逼近误差 $\delta_H(P_k, A) = O(k^{2/(1-q)})$，其中 $q$ 为目标数量，$k$ 为迭代次数。数值实验证实了该 $p$ 无关的收敛率。

Conclusion: 欧几里得情况（$p=2$）的最优率可推广至一般 $\ell_p$ 范数，且不损失收敛指数，仅增加一个维度相关的常数因子。

Abstract: We analyze convergence rates of norm-minimization-based outer approximation algorithms for convex vector optimization when the scalarization uses an $\ell_p$ norm with $p \in (1,\infty)$. While the Euclidean case ($p=2$) achieves the optimal rate $O(k^{2/(1-q)})$, the behavior under general $\ell_p$ norms has remained open. A direct approach via the modulus of smoothness yields only the weaker exponent $\min(p,2)$, which degrades for $1 < p < 2$. We prove that the Hausdorff approximation error satisfies $δ_H(P_k, A) = O(k^{2/(1-q)})$ for \emph{every} $p \in (1,\infty)$, where $q$ is the number of objectives and $k$ is the iteration count. The proof introduces a Euclidean intermediary technique that exploits the ambient inner product structure of $\R^q$ to obtain a quadratic bound on the hyperplane distance, bypassing the $\ell_p$ smoothness limitation; norm equivalence then converts this to any $\ell_p$ metric at the cost of only a dimension-dependent constant, not a loss of exponent. Numerical experiments confirm the $p$-independent rate predicted by the theory.

</details>


### [139] [PI-SONet: A Physics-Informed Symplectic Operator Network for Real-Time Optimal Control of Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14332)
*Alan John Varghese,Shanqing Liu,Paula Chen,Yaochen Zhu,Jérôme Darbon,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出PI-SONet框架，通过结构保持的算子学习解决高维最优控制问题，实现跨任务泛化的亚秒级推理，速度提升达10000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优控制求解器受维度诅咒困扰，且每个新问题都需要重新运行，无法实现实时控制高维多智能体系统。

Method: 结合潜在右空间求解器与条件辛算子，在计算高效的辅助空间中生成可处理的哈密顿轨迹，再变换回物理空间；训练单一算子近似PMP解映射，保持哈密顿结构。

Result: 在新问题上实现亚秒级推理，相比代表性基线方法获得最高10000倍加速。

Conclusion: 结构保持的神经算子为实现高维最优控制的可复用、实时替代模型提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Many real-life applications involve controlling high-dimensional multi-agent systems in real-time. Existing optimal control solvers often suffer from the curse-of-dimensionality and require complete rerunning for each new problem setting. We target nonconvex, nonlinear problems in 100s of dimensions by introducing PI-SONet (Physics-Informed Symplectic Operator Network), a structure-preserving operator learning framework for solving parameterized families of optimal control problems and their Pontraygin Maximum Principle (PMP) systems. PI-SONet combines a latent right-space solver with a conditional symplectic operator to produce tractable Hamiltonian trajectories in a computationally efficient auxiliary space and transform them back to physical space. This decomposition yields a \textit{single} trained operator that approximates the PMP solution map, inherently preserves Hamiltonian structure, and generalizes across unseen problem configurations. Unlike existing methods, which are fundamentally single-instance solvers, PI-SONet achieves sub-second inferences on new problem instances, equating to up to 10,000x speedup over representative baselines. These results suggest that structure-preserving neural operators provide a practical route toward reusable, real-time surrogates for high-dimensional optimal control.

</details>


### [140] [Convergence of difference inclusions via a diameter criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14345)
*Lexiao Lai,Mingzhi Song*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究由集值映射加噪声驱动的离散动力系统收敛性。通过控制迭代点间距离与连续势函数变化的关系，证明有界实现收敛到更新映射的极限点。提出分层下降框架，结合直径准则与直径估计，证明了常见一阶优化方法在步长O(1/k)下的收敛性，涵盖非精确和随机次梯度方法及动量方法，适用于可定义在多项式有界o-minimal结构中的局部Lipschitz目标函数。论证完全离散，不依赖连续时间逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散动力系统收敛性分析往往依赖连续时间逼近或对步长有严格限制，缺乏统一的离散分析框架来处理集值映射加噪声的复杂动力学，尤其对于一阶优化方法在步长O(1/k)下的收敛性缺乏系统性的理论保证。

Method: 1. 建立直径准则：有界实现收敛当且仅当迭代点间距离受连续势函数变化控制。2. 提出分层下降框架：将迭代投影到合适的分层结构上，追踪势函数沿迭代的下降（至多可和误差）。3. 结合直径准则与分层下降的直径估计，证明一阶优化方法的收敛性。

Result: 建立了离散动力系统收敛的直径准则与分层下降框架，证明了非精确次梯度法、随机次梯度法和动量方法在步长O(1/k)下收敛到更新映射的极限点，适用于可定义在多项式有界o-minimal结构中的局部Lipschitz目标函数。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个完全离散的、不依赖连续时间逼近的收敛性分析框架，统一推导了多种一阶优化方法在步长O(1/k)下的收敛保证，拓展了非光滑非凸优化的理论工具。

Abstract: We study discrete dynamics governed by a difference inclusion whose increment is the sum of a selection from a set-valued map and a noise term. For any bounded realization, convergence follows once the inter-iterate diameter is controlled by the variation of a continuous potential. The limit point is then critical for a scaled outer limit of the update map. To certify this diameter criterion, we develop a stratified descent framework: we project iterates onto a suitable stratification and track a potential that decreases up to a summable error. Combining the diameter criterion with a diameter estimate obtained from this framework yields convergence of common first-order optimization methods under step sizes of order $1/k$. The guarantees cover inexact and stochastic subgradient methods, as well as the momentum method, for locally Lipschitz objectives definable in polynomially bounded o-minimal structures. Our arguments are entirely discrete, with no appeal to continuous-time approximations.

</details>


### [141] [Equilibrium for Time-inconsistent Mean Field Games: A Systematic Analysis by Entropy Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14363)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Zhenhua Wang,Xiang Yu,Keyu Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间下一般时间不一致平均场博弈问题的均衡存在性与近似。通过引入消失熵正则化方法，将均衡表征为耦合的探索性均衡HJB方程与依赖于律的随机微分方程，利用Schauder不动点理论与抛物正则性估计证明正则化均衡的全局存在性，并通过紧性论证、Young测度技巧和对偶工具证明正则化均衡收敛到原问题的均衡。此外，提出策略迭代算法并证明其在短时间与弱终端交互条件下的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间不一致平均场博弈（如非指数贴现）会引致非局部均衡HJB系统，求解困难。现有方法对这类初时依赖型MFG的均衡存在性与近似缺乏系统性理论结果。

Method: 采用消失熵正则化方法；将正则化均衡表征为探索性均衡HJB方程与律依赖SDE的耦合系统；运用Schauder不动点定理与抛物正则性估计；利用紧性论证、Young测度技巧及对偶工具分析收敛性；提出策略迭代算法并分析收敛条件。

Result: 在温和假设下证明了正则化均衡的全局存在性；证明了正则化均衡沿子序列收敛到原始时间不一致MFG的均衡；在短时间与弱终端交互条件下证明了策略迭代算法的收敛性。

Conclusion: 熵正则化方法为求解时间不一致MFG提供了一种系统性的理论框架与数值途径，弥合了正则化近似与原均衡之间的理论鸿沟。

Abstract: This paper studies the existence and approximation of equilibria for general time-inconsistent mean field game (MFG) problems in the continuous-time setting. To handle the intricate nonlocal equilibrium Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (EHJB) system arising from initial-time dependence, such as non-exponential discounting, we develop a vanishing entropy regularization approach for solving the MFG. With entropy regularization, we first characterize the regularized equilibrium via a coupled exploratory equilibrium HJB (EEHJB) equation and a law-dependent stochastic differential equation. By exploiting Schauder fixed-point arguments and tailored parabolic regularity estimates in a suitable functional space involving both value functions and measure flows, we establish the global existence of regularized equilibria under mild assumptions. We next analyze convergence as the entropy regularization vanishes. By employing compactness arguments, Young measure techniques, and a duality tool for divergence-form Fokker-Planck equations, we prove that the regularized equilibria converge, up to subsequences, to an equilibrium of the original time-inconsistent MFG. Furthermore, under entropy regularization, we propose a policy iteration algorithm and establish its convergence under a short time horizon and weak terminal interaction conditions.

</details>


### [142] [Boundary null-controllability for the beam equation with classical structural damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14371)
*Sergei Avdonin,Julian Edward*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了区间(0,π)上带结构阻尼的梁方程在不同边界条件下的适定性和零可控性，证明当ρ≤2时对所有T>0零可控成立，当ρ>2时对几乎所有ρ成立但对(2,∞)中稠密子集失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究结构阻尼梁方程在边界控制下的零可控性，特别关注阻尼参数ρ对可控性的影响，填补该领域关于临界阻尼参数的理论空白。

Method: 利用Dirichlet Laplacian谱分解和半群理论建立适定性，通过矩量法将可控性问题转化为谱条件分析，结合解析函数和几乎周期函数理论处理参数ρ的临界情形。

Result: (1) 当ρ≤2时，对所有T>0零可控成立；(2) 当ρ>2时，零可控性对几乎所有ρ成立，但在(2,∞)中稠密子集上失败；(3) Neumann控制情形有类似结论。

Conclusion: 结构阻尼梁方程的零可控性由阻尼参数ρ决定：ρ≤2时完全可控，ρ>2时可控性表现出稠密性-几乎处处 dichotomy 现象，揭示了阻尼对边界可控性的非线性影响。

Abstract: Let $Δ$ be the Dirichlet Laplacian on the interval $(0,π)$, and let $T>0$. We prove a well-posedness results for the structurally damped beam equation $$u_{tt}+Δ^2 u-ρΔu_t=0, x\in (0,π),t>0$$ with various boundary conditions including $$ u(0,t)=u_{xx}(0,t)=0; u(π,t)=f(t),u_{xx}(π,t)=0, $$ and $f\in H_0^2(0,T)$ and appropriate initial conditions. Viewing $f$ as a control, we prove null controllability for all $ρ\leq 2$. For $ρ>2$, we show null controllability for arbitrary $T>0$ holds for almost all $ρ$, but fails for a dense subset of $(2,\infty)$.
  An analagous result is proven for Neumann control.

</details>


### [143] [On the Nature of Regularity Assumptions in Bilevel Optimization with Constrained Lower-level Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14409)
*Xiaotian Jiang,Chang He,Mingyi Hong,Shuzhong Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了双层优化中下层问题常用的正则性假设（LICQ、严格互补松弛条件、二阶充分条件），证明这些条件在**所有**上层变量x处成立是非常强的假设（非普适性），而仅在**几乎处处**成立则是弱的假设（普适性）。尽管两者仅差一个零测集，但这一差异在双层优化的理论和计算中带来了根本性困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在澄清双层优化中下层问题正则性假设的合理性，质疑"所有x处成立"这一常见假设是否过于严格，并分析不同强度假设对理论和计算的影响。

Method: 1. 通过刚性定理证明当条件在每点x成立时，下层问题的某些结构量必须保持不变；2. 构造反例展示这些不变量在不同x处存在差异；3. 利用随机扰动分析证明"几乎处处成立"是普适的（以概率1成立）。

Result: "处处成立"是非普适的：存在问题无论如何扰动都无法使条件在所有x处成立；"几乎处处成立"是普适的：随机扰动下以概率1成立。但两者之差虽仅是一个零测集，却给双层优化的理论和计算带来根本性困难。

Conclusion: 建议研究者在使用下层正则性假设时需谨慎区分"处处"与"几乎处处"的版本，后者更合理但前者的缺失会导致非平凡的理论和计算挑战。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the regularity assumptions commonly adopted in bilevel optimization with constrained lower-level problems, including the linear independence constraint qualification, the strict complementary slackness condition, and the second-order sufficient condition. These conditions are typically required to hold for the lower-level problem at every upper-level variable $x$. We first show that the requirement that these conditions hold at every upper-level variable $x$ is strong, in the sense that it is non-prevalent: there exist problems for which no sufficiently small perturbation of the lower-level objective and constraints can make the conditions hold at every $x$. To establish the result, we prove rigidity theorems showing that certain structural quantities of the lower-level problem must remain invariant across all $x$ whenever these conditions hold everywhere. We then construct explicit counterexamples in which these invariants differ between two values of $x$. In contrast, we show that the weaker requirement, that these conditions hold at almost every $x$, is a weak assumption, in the sense that it is prevalent: with probability one over a random perturbation of the lower-level objective and constraints, each condition holds at almost every $x$. We further analyze the gap between the two requirements. Although the ``every $x$'' and ``almost every $x$'' versions differ only on a measure-zero set, we show that this difference introduces fundamental difficulties in both theory and computation for bilevel optimization.

</details>


### [144] [OPTIMUS: Optimization Productivity Tool for Intelligent Management of Utilizable Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14430)
*Souvik Bhattacharyya,Nisha Singh,Salman Haider,Balaji Nagarajan,Ved Prakash Dwivedi,Nithin Surendran,Karthik Nair*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出OPTIMUS框架，将零售空间优化问题建模为线性二进制背包模型，结合动态规划与混合整数规划的两阶段方法，在多个部门和门店集群上实现平均11.8%的销售额提升和9.5%的利润提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售空间中有限的货架容量需要在多个货架陈列图（POG）之间进行分配，同时满足业务和运营约束。现有启发式方法存在结果方差大的问题，需要一种可重复、可解释且利润驱动的优化方案。

Method: 采用两阶段优化：第一阶段将品类选择建模为线性二进制背包问题，使用动态规划（DP）在O(nc)时间内获得精确且可复现的品类组合决策；第二阶段将品类决策输入到混合整数规划（MIP）模型中，进行货架层面的空间优化。

Result: 在跨多个部门和门店集群的十次优化运行中，OPTIMUS框架平均实现11.8%的销售额提升和9.5%的利润提升。

Conclusion: OPTIMUS提供了一个可扩展、可解释且利润驱动的企业级零售空间管理解决方案，通过精确的DP方法避免了启发式方法的方差问题。

Abstract: We study department-level retail space optimization, where limited bay capacity must be allocated among planograms (POGs) under business and operational constraints. The problem is formulated as a linear binary knapsack model, with potential SKUs treated as items characterized by space requirements and weighted value contributions from sales, margin, units, and assortment similarity. Dynamic Programming (DP) is employed to obtain exact and reproducible assortment decisions in O(nc) time, avoiding the variance inherent in heuristic approaches. These decisions are integrated with a second-stage bay optimization model formulated as a mixed-integer program. Evaluated end-to-end across ten optimization runs spanning multiple departments and store clusters, the OPTIMUS framework achieves an average sales lift of 11.8% and an average margin lift of 9.5%. Overall, OPTIMUS provides a scalable, interpretable, and profit-driven solution for enterprise-scale retail space management.

</details>


### [145] [Uncovering latent consensus in heterogeneous populations: The Mixture Linear Ordering Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14596)
*Juan A. Aledo,Concepción Domínguez,Juan de Dios Jaime-Alcántara,Mercedes Landete*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对经典线性排序问题无法处理异质群体偏好的局限，本文提出将人群划分为多个潜在群体，每个群体拥有独立的线性排序、规模及偏好结构，观测到的偏好矩阵为群体特定偏好的聚合结果。作者开发了混合整数规划公式，包括一种具有几何解释的紧致重构形式，并针对大规模问题设计了多起点交替方向启发式算法。实验表明，精确方法能有效恢复潜在群体结构，启发式算法则在更短时间内提供高质量解，在困难实例中有时甚至优于精确方法的最佳可行解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典线性排序问题假设人群是同质的，只能给出单一排序，但实际中观测到的偏好往往来源于多个潜在群体，每个群体有不同的排序模式。现有方法无法捕捉这种异质性，因此需要扩展模型以同时识别潜在群体及其各自的排序结构。

Method: 提出将总体划分为潜在群体的扩展线性排序模型，每个群体有各自的线性排序、相对大小和偏好结构；开发混合整数规划（MIP）公式及紧致重构形式（具有线性排序多面体内的几何解释）；设计多起点交替方向启发式算法，迭代更新群体排序和权重。

Result: 在合成数据上的计算实验表明，精确方法能够成功恢复潜在群体结构；所提启发式算法在显著缩短的计算时间内提供高质量解，在困难实例的时间限制内，有时甚至优于精确方法的最佳可行解。

Conclusion: 该扩展模型有效解决了异质群体偏好聚合问题，精确方法和启发式算法各有优势，分别适用于不同规模和难度的问题实例。

Abstract: The classical linear ordering problem seeks a single ranking representing a given preference matrix. While suitable for homogeneous populations, it fails when observed preferences arise from several latent groups with distinct ranking patterns. To address this limitation, we introduce an extension partitioning the population into latent groups, each characterized by its own linear order, relative size, and preference structure. The observed matrix is then explained as the aggregate outcome of these group-specific preferences. We develop mixed-integer programming formulations, including a compact reformulation yielding a geometric interpretation within the linear ordering polytope. Because exact solutions become computationally demanding for larger instances, we propose a multi-start alternating-direction matheuristic iteratively updating group rankings and weights. Computational experiments on synthetically generated instances, matching sizes typical in preference aggregation scenarios, demonstrate the effectiveness of the exact approach in successfully recovering the underlying groups. Furthermore, the proposed heuristic delivers high-quality solutions in substantially shorter times, occasionally improving upon the exact method's best incumbent in difficult instances within the imposed time limit.

</details>


### [146] [Optimal Asymptotic Rates for (Stochastic) Gradient Descent under the Local PL-Condition: A Geometric Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14663)
*Sebastian Kassing,Thomas Kruse*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析了在满足Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL)不等式和乘法梯度噪声模型下，随机梯度下降(SGD)和梯度下降(GD)的局部收敛行为，证明在可能非凸的条件下，(S)GD的渐近收敛率与强凸二次函数相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管SGD在机器学习中被广泛应用，但在非凸优化中，特别是满足PL条件且存在乘法梯度噪声的情况下，其局部收敛行为的理论分析尚不充分。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 利用PL条件的几何解释，对满足PL不等式的C²函数在乘法梯度噪声模型下进行理论分析，比较GD和SGD的局部收敛行为。

Result: 在可能非凸的设置下，(S)GD的渐近收敛率与强凸二次函数相匹配。
